### `assets/js/flows-status.js`, `assets/css/flows-status.css`, `tests/flows-status-render.mjs`

The owner-only Status page, `/flows/status/` (`statusPage`, a row in `server/routes/flows-pages.js` marked `ownerOnly`: a signed-in member who is not an owner gets the unknown-page 404, an anonymous visitor the sign-in page, and no switch opens it to members). It reads four routes and nothing else: `/api/flows/ready` (one readiness line: ready or the count of blockers, each with its sentence, and the US open in TRT with the minutes to it), `/api/rt/status` (rail mode, sockets, vendor calls a minute, degraded episode, kill switches, per topic last frame, lag, calls, failures), `/api/flows/meta` (the nightly run: written time, cards, health and the stage ledger) and `/api/flows/ai-usage` (the model day's calls, tokens and neurons by model). It makes no vendor call and no model call and adds no payload or global. Every time is written `HH:MM TRT` by a local formatter that adds three hours to the instant (Türkiye has no DST), with the date only when it is not today; a source that cannot be read, or a value it does not carry, is a dash with its reason, never zero. The Operations group of the sidebar shows only on this page. The suite is Chromium with `page.route` stubs, no server: the four routes and no others, the readiness line, the heading outline, TRT conversions, units, dashes for every missing case, no other zone named, no overflow at 320, 390 and 1440 px.

### `docs/flows-design-system.md`, `tests/flows-kit-render.mjs`

The Flows design system v2: the surface, elevation, focus, density, data-type and sequential tokens and the `.ds-` kit (module, state chip, button, row, signed figure) that sit in `assets/css/flows.css`, the fourteen module states (`loading`, `quiet`, `partial`, `pending`, `stale`, `withheld`, `unavailable`, `signedOut`, `quota`, `plan`, `held`, `live`, `fresh`, `closed`), the colour-vision rule (a pair under CIEDE2000 20 in any of three simulations carries a printed sign and a shape) and the forced-colors rules. Every added rule is a `:root` property or a `.ds-` selector, so no page changes until it adopts a class. The test serves the shipped stylesheets from disk on a fake origin and renders the kit at 320, 390, 768 and 1440 px: no overflow, text at 4.5:1 over its composited surface, a 44 px hit area by `elementFromPoint`, visible focus by Tab, the named states, forced colors, reduced motion and a coarse pointer; Chromium, no server.

Ticker chips point at the ids its hash jump already understands.

### `assets/js/flows-chart.js`

The Flows chart library and kernel (`FlowsUI.chart`: `mount`, `line`, `bars`, `diverging`, `heatmap`, `sparkline`, `gauge`, `payoff` and the primitives under them, plus the kernel: `kind(name, impl)` and `plot(host, spec)` over a closure `Map` registry (a duplicate name throws and the frozen object is never mutated), `scale` (linear, log, sqrt, band, session with closed hours compressed), `ticks` (labels through `F.unit` by kind name; a log domain with no positive part has none and one under a decade falls back to nice ticks), a two-dimensional `scrub` for bubble and scatter charts, a hidden description written from a kind's `data()` (the last value with its unit and time, then the low and the high), an optional table view and a provenance footer). The v1 calls are adapters over the same registry and render the same marks, held by node and attribute snapshots in `tests/fixtures-chart-kernel.json`. `dateTicks`, `fx1`, `tw` and `heightFor` are exported so that a controller keeps no copy of them (the ticker kept five), and `scrub` takes `ys`, `rs`, `yw` and `reach` for a chart whose marks are found in two dimensions, `first` to start the keyboard at the first mark, `xh` for the crosshair's opacity, `ariaHint` for the sentence after the label and, from a readout, `say` for the words announced. Split out of `flows-ui.js`. It re-publishes a new frozen `FlowsUI` with `chart` added, the way `flows-fresh.js` and `flows-rt.js` do, and `shared/flows-pages.js` emits it directly after `flows-ui.js` on the ten chart pages only (overview, the long and short boards, watch, desk, market, track, unusual, ticker, history, strategy); events, political, Ask and login never download it, so `FlowsUI.chart` is absent there. It duplicates the eight helpers `flows-ui.js` does not export (`paint`, `isoDay`, `AH`, `nextId`, `RAF`, `MON`, `spoken`, `moving`) rather than widening the frozen surface. Script order is load-bearing: a script that captured `window.FlowsUI` between the two would lose `chart`; `tests/flows-weight.mjs` and the worker and ticker contracts hold the order.

### `assets/js/flows-skyline.js`, `tests/flows-skyline-render.mjs`

The move skyline (F-95, placement 3) on the Market page. `FLOWS_SCENES` (`wrangler.toml` [vars], shipped `on`) is `on`, `owner` or off: the Worker passes `scenes` to `marketPage` when the switch is `on`, or is `owner` and the member is an owner (`FLOWS_RT_USERS`), and the page then emits the `mkSkylineCard` module and the `skyline` defer group, so a member's HTML carries neither. `flows-market.js` hands the universe it already reads to `FlowsUI.skyline.mount` when the module is within 200 px of the viewport; no new request or payload. `model(u)` is pure: `IV-days = chg / (iv30p / sqrt(252))` per priced name (the IV-days lens of `shared/flows-moves0.js`), prisms for the names at or beyond 2 IV-days, at most 40, a name with earnings inside 21 sessions marked as an event. A toggle (`FlowsUI.segmented`, arrow keys) sets what height shows. IV-days: the move itself, not a probability, no selection line, no error rate measured. How often: the count of days over the last year for that name as far out as the rung its move reached, read from the universe's `sig`, `f2` and `f3` (`sigmaProfile` through `buildUniverse`; a null cell is absence): 3x when the absolute change over `sig` is at least 3, 2x from 2, none under 2. N is 252 over the count, floored at 250 as `rarityText` does (a zero count or N over 250 is `rarer than 1 in 250`, drawn at the floor under a line above the prism), and height is `log10(N) / log10(250)` of the span on a labelled log axis ticked 1 in 2, 5, 10, 25, 50, 100 and the floor. A name with no scale, under 2 typical days, or with earnings inside 21 sessions is not drawn there and is named in a note; the option is off when none has a count. No p and no sigma wording; `assets/js` imports nothing, so the browser carries its own copy of the reader rule and the test holds every sentence equal to `rarityText`. Direction is on the colour channel (`--up-mark`, `--down-mark`) and in a triangle and sign in the tables; an event prism is paler with a dashed top. The three tallest in the active encoding (largest IV-days, or rarest) are called out with a box in the right margin (triangle, ticker, signed IV-days, the reader-rule sentence or the reason there is none): their prisms sit on the back edge, where nothing stands behind them, and each leader rises to its own lane above the skyline and runs to its stacked box, so none crosses a prism or another leader. Canvas 2D, a 2:1 dimetric projection, no label on any other prism; from 640 px of module width, below that the table of the three and the table of the names (a How often column, sorted by the active encoding) stand in. `tests/flows-skyline-render.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route`, no server) holds heights to IV-days and to `log10(N)` against independent sums and `rarityText`, the ticks, the toggle by key and click, the callouts and `labelProblems` at 640 to 1440 px and on 40 prisms, the absence note, a `buildUniverse` payload read back, pixel colour and tokens, the empty and unpublished states, no overflow at 320, 390 and 1440, and seventeen planted wrong builds.

| `assets/js/flows-skyline.js` |  | flows-skyline-render |
| `tests/flows-skyline-render.mjs` |  | flows-skyline-render |

### `assets/js/flows-relations.js`, `tests/flows-relations-render.mjs`

The Relations view (F-98, placement 6), beside the move skyline on the Market page under the same `FLOWS_SCENES` switch. Names sit on a ring by sector (the nightly universe's `sec`, largest by market cap first, at most 60), with a table of the names by sector as the alternative. `mount(host, universe, graph)` draws an edge only if it passed its test: a layer is `T` (moved together beyond the market) or `C` (residual partial correlation), an edge needs `passed === true`, a finite bootstrap stability of 0.5 or more and two names on the ring, and is drawn at one of three opacity steps (0.9 and up, 0.7 to 0.9, 0.5 to 0.7) with its stability in a table row; one layer is shown at a time and the draw lists are never merged; any other layer id, the flow layer included, is ignored. No relations payload exists on main (the statistics of F-80 are not built), so `flows-market.js` passes `null` and the module shows the ring and the text `No tested links yet`; a graph whose edges all fail says `No stable links this week`. Communities and the live co-occurrence light are not built. `tests/flows-relations-render.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route`, no server) holds the draw lists and opacity steps to a hand-worked graph, the pixel at each curve's midpoint, the layers apart, the empty states as text, no overflow at 320, 390 and 1440, and six planted wrong builds.

### `assets/js/flows-moves.js`, `assets/css/flows-moves.css`, `tests/flows-moves-render.mjs`, `tests/lib/moves-fixture.mjs`

The Moves page (`/flows/moves/`, F-57), a one-screen field of every name in the nightly universe: `universe?cols=t,sec,chg,iv30p,sig,f2,f3,ed,mcap` (plus the live roster's `px` strips when their session is newer). Across is the move in the name's own typical days (`chg / sig`, `sig` the nightly's typical daily move), up is the move as a multiple of the implied day (`abs(chg) / (iv30p / sqrt(252))`, the prior close's 30-day IV; the one page-side division, with `shared/flows-moves0.js` as its oracle). Each dot is a triangle for a riser, an inverted triangle for a faller and a circle for a flat name, its area the market cap (square root scale, 2.5 px floor, the smallest size for a name with no cap), ringed when earnings are within 21 sessions; SPY and QQQ are fixed squares kept whatever the filter; the axes run to the 99.5th percentile of the drawn names (at least 3, capped at 12 and 10) and a name past them is drawn at the edge and counted in the foot. Dashed lines at 2 and 3 typical days and at 1 times implied are reference marks, not a test; no boundary curve, q or flagged set exists, because no joint test is published. Filters (Up, Down, Earnings week, a sector, Clear) fade the other dots and re-rank the lists; Freeze holds the read, prints the time in TRT and applies the held one on Resume; the order also holds while the pointer or focus is in the lists. Large now ranks names outside the earnings window by IV-days; Earnings within 30 days ranks the others apart; rows show a short rarity chip and the whole sentence in the title and the drill panel, only through the `rarityText` rules of `shared/flows-targets.js` (a count of 252 from `f2` or `f3`, the 1-in-250 floor, never a model probability) and only from 2 typical days. Show more opens a dialog sized to the viewport. The drill panel gives the move against its typical and implied day, how often, the card's last 21 closes and context (conviction, IV rank, dealer book, latest flow) read lazily from `GET /api/flows/card?t=` after first paint with a Retry, and the muted note `Significance test: not yet published.`; the info dialog lists what is not built (a significance test, parts of the move, flow clusters, replay). One screen at 1440x820, 1024x768 and 390x844 with no scroll and no inner scroll; a phone switches Field, Lists and Detail; arrow keys move between the sixty largest dots and the pins, Enter opens the ticker page, a tap selects the nearest dot within 22 px. Copy is held to fourteen words a sentence and none of unusual, alert, screen, rare, sigma or radar. `assets/js` imports nothing, so the page carries its own copy of the reader rule and the suite holds every sentence equal to `rarityText` for all 253 counts at both levels. No model, no vendor call, no new payload, no new global (`__MovesTest` exists only where there is no document). Gated by `FLOWS_MOVES` in `wrangler.toml` (shipped `on`; `owner` when unset, `on`, `off`; anything else fails closed for a member, and a refused request is the unknown-page 404). The suite is Chromium with `page.route` stubs, a synthetic universe (`tests/lib/moves-fixture.mjs`, no vendor data) and no server: zero scroll and nothing clipped in every panel, filter and dialog at three sizes, each dot's position, radius, shape and ring against an independent computation, lists, filters, Freeze, the live roster, the drill panel, a failed card and its Retry, the keyboard and a tap, and twenty-one planted wrong builds that each fail.

### `assets/js/flows-political.js`, `assets/css/flows-political.css`, `tests/flows-political-render.mjs`

The Political page (`/flows/political/`), stock first and one screen with no page scroll at 1440x820, 1024x768 and 390x844. Rows are tickers read from the one `GET /api/flows/political` row; people appear only as initials of evidence. Left panel: tabs Accumulating, Reducing and Convergence (counts with funds hidden; `FUND_TICKERS` behind a toggle), a Map or List switch and a Dollars or Filers switch. The map is a squarified treemap whose tile area is the square root of disclosed dollars (bought plus sold band midpoints; `treemapWeight`) or filers plus a dollar tiebreak, at most 18 tiles (12 on a phone), each with a ring of the median lag over the window (`blindShare`) and a state from `netBounds` and `stateOf`: low bound = bought low minus twice the sold midpoint, high bound = bought high minus an eighth of it, firm accumulating only when the low bound is above zero and no band is open, and no name is ever firm reducing. Right panel: the newest filings that name a stock, with a Show all dialog of all of them. Bottom rail for the selected name: disclosed dollars with range, breadth and median lag, the traded-versus-filed strip with the typically-unfiled band, Flows context and evidence. Flows context is one lazy `GET /api/flows/card?t=` per selected name, debounced 220 ms and cached, reading conviction, IV rank, dealer gamma book and flow, the last net premium and the price since the newest buy; an absent card or field is an em dash with its reason and a failure offers Retry. Phones switch between Stocks, Filings and Detail. Arrow keys move between tiles by geometry, Enter opens the ticker page. Only tokens, no colour literal. `tests/flows-political-render.mjs` (Chromium, `page.route` stubs on a synthetic payload of the real shape, no server) holds the scroll, areas, states, rings, strip, keyboard, card states and copy to independent computations and fails ten planted wrong builds.

### `scripts/approval-scene.mjs`, `scripts/approval-worlds.mjs`, `scripts/approval-frames.mjs`, `scripts/approval-checks.mjs`, `tests/fixtures-approval/*`, `tests/approval-frames.mjs`

The approval render set of the Frontier programme (F-02): six placements (the Home network with the seam at the Name layer, the Moves page, the skyline, the terrain, the surface, Relations) rendered from fixtures at 1440x900 and 390x844 with `node scripts/render-preview.mjs --approval [--capture] --out <dir>`; `--capture` also records the 20-second Live and Ambient capture. The scene modules are pure (no DOM, no Node API) so the capture page inlines `approval-scene.mjs` and runs the same code in the browser. The fixtures are `placements.json` (what each placement is for and its use test), `world.json` (the sample worlds), `copy.json` (the five canonical strings, the Live and Ambient sentence, the seam sentence), `superseded-frames.json` (the 35 frames the 2026-10-05 19:20 direction replaced, by file name), `answers.json` (one slot per placement and OD-V1's, every value `null` until the owner answers) and `gates.json` (the rows that name OD-39 and the placements each waits for); `gateOf` resolves a row to `gated`, `changes` or `released` and a null answer is never read as a yes. `tests/approval-frames.mjs` (Chromium, no server) holds the DOM text lint (no "at ask" or "at bid", no "NOW", no FDR in a heading, no Unusual or Radar in a nav label or title, header sentence at most 14 words, chip at most 3), the canonical strings verbatim in the Moves frames, the Home network's draw list (no curve through a Name node, no pulse across two bands, conservation, one scale for sphere area and band width, no chip on a sphere), the skyline's label placer against its prisms, the terrain's signs, the surface's total variance, Relations' stability steps and the capture (Live still between arrivals, Ambient swaying), each with a negative fixture. Nothing under `assets/` or `shared/` is part of it.

### `docs/launch-checklist.md`, `scripts/launch-readiness.mjs`, `tests/launch-readiness.mjs`

The go/no-go list for the first paying member as a readback: ten lines in parts, each a repository check (config, schema, routes, source), a dated readback recorded in the checklist's table, or a part dropped by an owner decision. `node scripts/launch-readiness.mjs` prints which lines are true and exits 1 until all are. The test holds the checklist's parts table equal to the script's, every recorded row valid, and the admission rule: no paid surface (`shared/plans.js`, a `subscriptions` or `billing_events` table, a `/api/billing/` route, `FLOWS_BILLING_MODE` other than `off`) while a line is false.

## tests

Suites, helpers and fixtures.

### `tests/route-matrix.mjs`, `tests/fixtures-route-matrix.json`

The Worker's whole routing surface in process, with no server: every route literal and prefix family, as anonymous, Flows member, Flows owner and Lab user, under six methods (plus the rail with `PULSE` absent, the switch off and the rail on, and cross-origin and owner-mismatch mutations), on a pinned clock, a fake `ASSETS`, a refusing `fetch` and `tests/lib/d1-fake.mjs`. Status, `Allow`, `Content-Type`, `Cache-Control`, `Location`, `error.code`, the seven security headers, the `X-Fresh-*` set and the cookie names are compared with the snapshot; a behaviour change regenerates it with `node route-matrix.mjs --write` in a pull request that moves no code. The fake D1 cannot return `RETURNING` rows, so the `DELETE /api/progress` cell is a 500 on the fake only. It also holds `worker.js` to one top-level `const state = createState()` and no other top-level mutable state, and `server/` (when it exists) to none.

| File | Exports | Suites |
|---|---|---|
| `tests/fixtures-route-matrix.json` |  | route-matrix |
| `tests/route-matrix.mjs` |  | route-matrix |

### `tests/flows-engine-budget.mjs`, `tests/flows-d1-budget.mjs`, `tests/lib/d1-budget.mjs`

The engines' D1 budget as a contract, in rows, with no server. `tests/lib/d1-budget.mjs` holds the caps (100,000 rows written and 5,000,000 read a day on Workers Free), the cron firings expanded from `wrangler.toml` (a weekday is 108 Tier 1, 108 focus, 48 housekeeping and 48 summary firings, a weekend day the last two), the per-firing ceilings of each job, the Tier 2 pass, the nightly's keys, the rail's roster loads, the planned writers and readers with the plan row that builds each, and `D1_USERS`, the modules that prepare a statement. `flows-engine-budget` drives the real code on the counting fake: every one of a weekday's 312 `scheduled` firings through `worker.js` on one database with Tier 2 passes between them, one ingest of each nightly key class, the roster batch, and the focus tick's held-row query, which it also runs with counting SQL functions to settle the inference (SQLite visits each held name once and re-runs the inner field scan once per name, stopping at the px field: 44 elements against the fake's bound of 641 rows: 22 names and the 28 fields of a name's strip). The fake (`tests/lib/d1-fake.mjs`) now prices `json_each(payload, ...) AS r`, bare or aliased, over the rows a statement names, and a json_each nested in a correlated subquery once for each element of the scan it sits under. A ceiling must hold and must not be loose. `flows-d1-budget` sums every declared writer and reader against the caps (about 13,000 written and 336,000 read, with every firing counted as a cold start and the planned rows included) and fails when a module in the Worker's closure prepares a statement that no declaration prices. The observer (P1-25) is declared, not measured: its ceiling and `shared/flows-engine-verdicts.js` must arrive together. Whether D1 bills `json_each` elements and DDL as rows is not measurable on a local database, so both are counted.

The flow network on the real unusual page in Chromium against `page.route` stubs, no server: layer counts and premium conservation through every layer, hop and node against an independent sum in Node, the ranking on screen at rest, at the reachable yaw and pitch limits, after reordering data and under reduced motion, Live at rest with a paint census of zero over 10 s, the arrival that lights only its own bands and expires after five minutes, the Ambient button, tag, legend, remembered choice and events, the owner default and the closed-session default, no `lighter` pass and no sway in Live, contracts in the tooltips, mouse and touch orbit (clamps at the angle where a label would collide, inertia, recentring, a vertical touch still scrolling the page), hit targets after a rotation, the painter's order, hover, row and keyboard highlighting, the table and the canvas label, the freshness pill, the Expiry output, a flare on a new window, focus kept through a rebuild, pause when hidden or off screen, reduced motion, the pending, empty, failed and unread states, no overflow, no label or caption collision and every label within half a pitch of its own sphere at 320, 390, 700, 768, 1024, 1280 and 1440 px in both outputs over a 9 by 5 yaw and pitch grid, along sixteen points of the sway path and where a long drag stops, and the v3 audit (`auditFn`, run in the page on `net.scene()`) at rest at 1440, 1024 and 390 (columns or rows equally spaced within 1 px, every stack centred on the midline within 1 px, chips on one x edge per column, sphere radius, band width and each node's outgoing slice sum on the stated scale, the same total on every header, the legend's reference circles and swatches on that scale) and over a 0 to 40 s sweep at 0.1 s (no chip over a sphere or a chip, nothing outside the canvas); a readout and header clearance audit (a hover readout at least 12 px clear of every header and inside the canvas) and eight mutants of the served module (equal spheres, bands not by premium, unequal columns, an off-centre stack, a ragged chip edge, a header with another total, headers up under the readout, headers down on the spheres) that the audit must fail; a delayed-font block at 320 and 700 px (the woff2 held back 3 s, then labels, layout and collisions re-checked once it lands); at 320 and 390 px the legend's three circles on one row and its three swatches on one row, and no table header with a scrollWidth over its clientWidth; breathing between 1.00 and 1.03, Freeze, `destroy()` leaving no observer or listener of its own, no retained heap after 3,000 frames and under 3 KiB allocated a frame; CPU per frame at 120 or more bands and 200 pulses, also with the raster forced to finish (a pixel read after every draw), held as a ratio to a reference canvas workload timed in the same interleaved windows rather than as milliseconds, since an absolute figure flaked at load 10 on identical code (limit 2.5 times the reference, floor 12 ms; 1.4 to 1.5 measured). `NET_SHOTS=<dir>` saves screenshots.

### `tests/truth/*.mjs`, `tests/flows-truth.mjs`, `tests/rt-market-sim.mjs`, `tests/gen-truth-fixtures.py`, `tests/fixtures-truth.json`

The one synthetic market the statistics are judged on. `rng.mjs` is sfc32 seeded from sha256 (equal word for word to the Python twin in the generator script); `worlds.mjs` holds W1 (the production GARCH-skew-t class), W1d (the dependent daily world), W3 and W3b (marked Hawkes flow, negative-binomial flow counts with day effects and weather), W4 (look-ahead bait), W5 (open-interest lag), W6 (overfitting null), W7 (calibration worlds) and W8 (overlapping horizons); `intraday.mjs` is W9, the 670-name minute world, and W9o, its out-of-family preset; `runner.mjs` refuses to accept a result from a well-specified cell and to judge with no out-of-family world; `reference.mjs` holds the reference implementations the planted wrong builds are made from; `mutants.mjs` loads a copy of a module through a data URL with exact-once string transforms (production is never edited) and `detectors.mjs` holds the detector each mutant must fail and the correct build must pass. `flows-truth.mjs` prints the kill matrix; `FLOWS_TRUTH_DEEP=1` runs ten times the replications on fresh seeds and prints `FLAG` lines without failing. `rt-market-sim.mjs` serves one seeded event log as the vendor's REST routes and its socket protocol on a virtual clock, with the chaos knobs (drops, stalls, duplicates, reorders within 500 ms, malformed frames, caps, 429, lag spikes, clock skew, a silent channel, a missing periscope frame). The fixtures' provenance records the numpy and scipy versions and the sha256 of the script, and the suite fails when the script changed without a regenerate. `W1_BANDS` and the `w1-jumps-production-clip-in-filter` expectation pin today's winsorising limit (K 6): a change to it (F-22, or P3-16's K 6 to 10, which moves the measured tail ratio to about 0.965) fails `w1-bands` and the baseline of the `garchRecovery` mutants, and must re-pin `W1_BANDS` and flip that expectation in the same change. Most planted wrong builds mutate `reference.mjs` or `detectors.mjs`, not production code; each later row retargets its mutants at the production path it adds, one line in `mutants.mjs`.

### `tests/flows-alert-grid.mjs`, `tests/truth/alert-reference.mjs`, `tests/truth/alert-grid.mjs`, `tests/truth/alert-detectors.mjs`, `tests/truth/alert-mutants.mjs`, `tests/truth/alertsim-evidence.mjs`

The alert decision grid (F-14), the acceptance contract every alert construction is judged by. `alert-reference.mjs`: BY with N fixed at the open on running p that only fall (so the union over a session is the BY set at the close), the null-cell bank, and the two registered constructions with the same 1, 5, 15 and 60 minute windows (A, a calibrated session-maximum law read through the cell nu-hat minus 1 and 1.5 s-hat; B, a per-increment transformed normal mixture, the overnight gap included, scaled by e to the c s-hat). `alert-grid.mjs`: the cells (the exact world; heterogeneous tails with scale error 0.1, 0.2 and 0.3 and random-sign sectors; the overnight-gap world; W9o with its nightly measurement and paused buckets), covered and report-only steps, the session-clustered FDR bound, the J x D power cells and their pre-registered weights, the BH challenger on residualised p, the macro-day stress cell and `decide()`, the registered rule. `alertsim-evidence.mjs` carries the alertsim4 and alertsim3 outputs the rule is also run on. `alert-mutants.mjs` plants five wrong builds through `mutants.mjs` and `alert-detectors.mjs` holds the detector each must fail. `FLOWS_TRUTH_DEEP=1` runs 670 names on fresh seeds and prints `FLAG` lines without failing.

### `tests/gen-har-fixtures.py`, `tests/fixtures-har.json`

### `tests/lab-write-contract.mjs`

The Lab write limiter and the attempt ledgers with no server: `worker.js` in Node over `node:sqlite`, a counting `LAB_WRITE` fake (the 31st mutation in a window is a 429 with `Retry-After`, every mutation route is behind it, reads are not, a missing or failing limiter admits the write, a refused request makes no D1 trip), the attempt statements planned by primary key only, the `received_at` indexes and the 03:00 ET prune.

| File | Exports | Suites |
|---|---|---|
| `tests/lab-write-contract.mjs` |  | lab-write |

### `tests/flows-pages-render.mjs`, `tests/fixtures-pages-render-baseline.json`

Every page function in `FLOWS_PAGES` (fifteen) rendered in Chromium at 320, 390 and 1440 px against the pipeline's dry-run emit, served from `page.route` with no server (about 2.5 minutes, 60 s of it the emit). Hard assertions: one `h1`, no skipped heading level, no page error, and the Ask dock (a dialog with `aria-modal`, nothing outside it focusable, focus inside and the main region `inert` when narrow; a complementary landmark beside a usable page when wide; Escape closes and focus returns to the tab). Counted against the committed baseline, which may fall but not rise: axe-core `color-contrast`, unnamed controls, structure rules, horizontal-overflow pixels, targets under 24 px, and (coarse pointer) targets under 44 px. `PAGES_RENDER_UPDATE=1` rewrites the baseline; the metrics go to `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY`; no screenshot is kept. `axe-core` is pinned in `tests/package.json`; where `tests/node_modules` predates the pin, `NODE_PATH` can point at a directory that has it.

| File | Exports | Suites |
|---|---|---|
| `tests/fixtures-pages-render-baseline.json` |  | flows-pages-render |
| `tests/flows-pages-render.mjs` |  | flows-pages-render |

### `tests/flows-deadline-render.mjs`

The Flows fetch wrapper's deadline (`bounded` in `assets/js/flows-ui.js`): every GET to `/api/flows/*` that carries no signal of its own is aborted at 20 s, or at its `deadlineMs`, and the timer stays armed until `json()`, `text()`, `blob()` or `arrayBuffer()` settles, so a body that stalls after its headers is bounded too; a caller's signal wins, a POST and any other URL are untouched, and a 401 still hangs. Home and Ask pass `deadlineMs: 15000`, the desk and strategy 45000. Chromium against a loopback HTTP server that hangs, or stalls after headers, and a paused page clock stepped with `runFor`: twelve pages each show a failure state, and a source scan holds every `AbortSignal.timeout` and `.any` in `assets/js` behind `?.`. A page test that jumps the fake clock past 20 s with a fetch in flight now aborts it, so such tests step the clock in 10 s pieces.

| File | Exports | Suites |
|---|---|---|
| `tests/flows-deadline-render.mjs` |  | flows-deadline-render |

### `tests/flows-chart-kernel.mjs`, `tests/fixtures-chart-kernel.mjs`, `tests/fixtures-chart-kernel.json`, `tests/lib/chart-page.mjs`

The chart kernel with no server. A `vm` block holds the scales (the old `lin` closure to the last digit, log, sqrt, band, session and their inverses), ticks (a log domain from zero included) and the registry; a Chromium block over a hermetic page (fixed colour tokens, no stylesheet) and a second with the shipped stylesheets holds U+2212 on negatives, the em dash for a null, a gap that is not zero, a zero that is not positive, the keyboard reaching every mark of each kind, a description that names the last value with its unit, the table and provenance, an update that keeps the crosshair and does not animate, and a frame-cost ratio against the v1 redraw. The 19 v1 calls are recorded at 390 and 960 px with the readout after a key press; `CHART_GOLDEN=write node flows-chart-kernel.mjs` rewrites the snapshot, which a reviewer must read before it is committed.

### `tests/lib/affected.mjs`, `tests/ci-affected.mjs`

Affected-suite selection for pull requests: `node run.mjs --affected <base-ref>` (from `tests/`) reads `git diff --name-only <base>...HEAD`, joins each changed file to the suites whose import closure (the registry's walker, static and literal dynamic imports) or `covers` globs reach it, and runs those plus the whole `fast` group; `--affected-list` prints the selection without running, and with `--shard i/n` the shard keeps only its affected suites. Any doubt selects everything: an unreadable or empty diff, a file that maps to no suite (a deletion included), a file under `tests/lib/`, `tests/run.mjs`, the lockfile and `tests/package.json`, `.github/`, `wrangler*.toml`, `worker.js`, `.assetsignore` or `_headers`, or a file that reaches more than 40% of the suites. The workflow's `plan` job runs the list on pull requests only; the push-to-main run, the weekly schedule and manual dispatch never select, and a partial pull-request run writes no `tested-tree` status, so `scripts/ci-reuse.mjs` can never skip the push gate on a partial run. `ci-affected` proves exact selections on a miniature repository (leaf, transitive, literal dynamic import, covers, registry file), every fallback, the real `git diff`, and that selectors ignoring `covers` or transitive imports fail the same expectations.

| File | Exports | Suites |
|---|---|---|

### `wrangler.staging.toml`, `scripts/ops-probe.mjs`, `scripts/staging-load.mjs`, `tests/staging-contract.mjs`

The staging Worker's complete, separate configuration (own D1 and `PULSE`, namespaces 2101 to 2106, no cron, no route, no AI binding, live dispatch off, no secret); the external prober (`probe`: `/api/health` and one page, two failures to alert, a webhook from the environment) and the `smoke` checks; the 20-member poll and socket load test, which refuses production and prices a Free run first. `staging-contract` runs the configuration through wrangler's own reader and the tools against fakes, with no server. DEPLOY.md 10.6.
